# NEOTH — Road to 1.0 GOLD (Leitlinien / Master Plan)

---

## 0. START HERE (fresh session)

**What is NEOTH?** A local-first sovereign personal-AI daemon written in Rust. Single operator, runs on the operator's machine. Core primitives: hemisphere council (Left/Right/Cerebellum provider routing), append-only WAL audit log (cryptographically signed frames), consent-gated tool execution, skills system, memory tiers (hot/warm/cold + groundtruth), channel adapters (Telegram, WhatsApp, Slack, Discord, Keet/Pears), and a coding-buddy subsystem. The binary is `neothd` (daemon + CLI). A GUI companion `neothd-gui` runs Slint. Architecture spec: `PLAN/00_DESIGN_v1.1_FINAL.md`.

**Current HEAD:** `3df06b5` · branch `main` · clean.

**What this file is:** The single source of truth for every task needed to reach the 1.0 GOLD release tag. Read it from top to bottom. Pick the first `[ ]` OPEN task in a workstream, do it, flip it to `[x]`, update `PLAN/PROGRESS_v1_0.md` in the same commit, push. Repeat.

**Rule: OPEN = `[ ]`, DONE = `[x]`. Nothing else.** No `[~]`, no "deferred", no "partial" markers. If something is half-shipped, the shipped part gets `[x]` and the remainder gets `[ ]`.

**How to pick work:** Go workstream by workstream (WS-A through WS-H). Within a workstream, prerequisites come first (tasks are ordered). Batch all work on a single file using the FILE-BATCH INDEX in section 4.

**Source artifacts:** All audit findings are in `REVIEWS/_gold_audit/`. The 6 agent audit files and `_wiring_results.md` are the primary input. Research files for Gold-TODO features are in `REVIEWS/_gold_audit/research/`.

## 1. Build & Test Recipe

Windows + MSVC only. `cargo` must run inside vcvars64 via a throwaway `SRC/_run.bat`:

```bat
@echo off
set "PATH=C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer;%PATH%"
call "C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft Visual Studio\2022\BuildTools\VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat"
set "INCLUDE=%INCLUDE%;C:\Program Files (x86)\Windows Kits\10\Include\10.0.22621.0\ucrt"
set "LIB=%LIB%;C:\Program Files (x86)\Windows Kits\10\Lib\10.0.22621.0\ucrt\x64"
cd /d C:\Users\Shadow-PC\CascadeProjects\AGENTER\SRC
cargo %*
echo RUN_EXIT=%ERRORLEVEL%
```

Invoke: `cmd.exe //c 'C:\Users\Shadow-PC\CascadeProjects\AGENTER\SRC\_run.bat' test -p neothd --lib`

Background it; read `RUN_EXIT=`; `tail` masks exit code — never pipe on gate runs.

**Delete `_run.bat` before committing.**

- vcvars MUST be UNREDIRECTED — `>nul` breaks `cl.exe` → cc-rs `ToolNotFound` on ring/zstd-sys.
- `neothd-gui` is a BIN crate → `cargo test -p neothd-gui` (no `--lib`). os-clipboard feature: `--features os-clipboard`.
- Clippy gate: `clippy -p neothd --tests --no-deps -- -D warnings`
- CLI doc drift: adding/removing a CLI command/flag → `NEOTH_REGEN_CLI_DOCS=1 cargo test -p neothd --lib cli_commands_md_is_up_to_date`
- Per-item workflow: flip `[ ]`→`[x]` in `PROGRESS_v1_0.md` AND this file in the SAME commit; conventional commit; tests green before claiming done; `git push`.

## 2. WAL / Code Gotchas

- **0xA band FULL.** Free WAL slots: `0x5D-5F`, `0x69-6F`, `0x78-7F`, `0x85-8F`, `0x9D-9F`, `0xBE-BF`, `0xDD-DF`, `0xF6+`. (GR-078: 0x52-5C, 0xCE-CF and 0xDB-DC are ASSIGNED — removed from the free list; verified against wal/events.rs.)
- **Adding a WAL event = 5 sites:** const + compile-time band-assert + `EVENT_NAME_TABLE` + `all_event_codes_are_unique` + emit-site; plus `immediate_sync` default.
- **Exhaustive `Action` matches** live in `permissions/mod.rs` (`evaluate_strict/standard/elevated/full` + `lease_scope_for`) — adding a new `Action` variant requires updating all four arms.
- **Pastejack guards** must be `chars()`-based, not byte-slice based.
- **release.yml** uses default features — a default-OFF feature ships only if `--features X` added to the native step.
- **`#[test]` calling `wal::writer::spawn`** panics — use `#[tokio::test]`.
- **vcvars `>nul` breaks cl.exe** — arboard Wayland feat = `wl-clipboard-rs`; always unredirected.
- **Workflow `agent()` without `schema:`** eaten by fp-check Stop hook — use `schema:` or `SendMessage`.
- **`wal::scan` v2/zstd divergence in production:** `rollback.rs`, `indexer.rs`, `undo.rs`, `updater.rs` use bare `SEGMENT_HEADER_LEN` with no zstd decompress — silently skip compressed segments.
- **SQLite mutex over `.await`:** `ConnBorrow::Shared(shared.lock().await)` held for full LLM pipeline — `#[allow(clippy::await_holding_lock)]` is a suppression, not a fix.
- **Raw byte-slice panics:** `&s[..N]` where `s` has multibyte UTF-8 causes panic — use `chars().take(N).collect()` or `floor_char_boundary`.
- **0xDB, 0xDC, 0xDD** are claimed by multiple research plans — coordinate before assigning new WAL events in the 0xD* band.

_Progress log: 2026-06-06 — GOLD-SEC-01/17 (relay auth + cap) · GOLD-SEC-02/27 (backup zip-slip + creds opt-out) · GOLD-SEC-03 (skills installer id traversal) · GOLD-SEC-04/28/34 (LIKE-escape + GDPR idx_profile cascade) · GOLD-SEC-05/06 (blocking-async offload) · GOLD-SEC-07 (OMI SSRF allowlist) · GOLD-SEC-08 (rate-limit bounded map) · GOLD-SEC-09 (canonical cloud-egress classifier) · GOLD-SEC-10 (self-update signature default) · GOLD-SEC-11 (decompression-bomb caps) · GOLD-SEC-12/21 (browser-cred scrub-on-drop) · GOLD-SEC-13 (un-audited os fail-closed) · GOLD-SEC-14 (dream/test-log redact+0600) · GOLD-SEC-15 (atomic owner-only creds write) · GOLD-SEC-19 (MCP sanitizer replace-all, UTF-8-safe) · GOLD-SEC-22 (Gemini key → header)._

**Verdict:** NEOTH has a strong security/crypto core (ed25519-signed WAL, consent gates, GDPR forget, PII redaction), but the surface over-promises on several fronts: migration (`apply` is a `bail!`), device-sync (memory ingest silently dropped), GUI (6 of 10 settings tabs are stubs), local TTS (deferred). Real exploitable bugs exist on operator paths: zip-slip in backup restore, path traversal in skill installer, LIKE wildcard mass-delete in GDPR forget, blocking code on async executor, and self-reported test results auto-promoting tasks. Both reviews are folded in: beta @ `35c94d2` (all A-01..A-10 still true); Roman @ `3ef9771` (14 net-new findings, CR-002 HLC is fixed, all others stand).

## WS-A — Security Hardening (GOLD-SEC-NN)

External/unauthenticated exploits first, then operator-data-loss, then defense-in-depth.

- [x] **GOLD-SEC-01** Add bearer-token check at the top of `route()` in neoth-relay before dispatching any POST/GET branch, or gate public-bind with mandatory `--token` flag — *files:* `SRC/neoth-relay/src/serve.rs`, `SRC/neoth-relay/src/main.rs` — *test:* unauthenticated request to public-bind returns 401 — *origin:* A-01 — ✅ **DONE:** `route()` auth-gate (constant-time compare, checked before dispatch incl. unknown paths) + `extract_bearer` + `public_bind_requires_token` predicate + fail-closed startup bail on non-loopback bind without `--token`/`NEOTH_RELAY_TOKEN`; token never logged. 49 tests / 0 fail, clippy `-D warnings` clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-02** Before `entry.unpack(&dest)` in backup restore, canonicalize `dest` and bail if it does not start with `target_home` to prevent zip-slip path traversal — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/daemon/backup.rs` — *test:* tar with `../../../tmp/pwned` entry is rejected — *origin:* A-06 — ✅ **DONE:** added `safe_join` (rejects `..`/absolute/root components → always contained) used for every restore entry, PLUS reject symlink/hard-link entries outright (NEOTH backups are regular files+dirs only). Tests: `safe_join_allows_normal_paths_and_rejects_escapes` + `restore_rejects_symlink_entry` (tar-rs itself refuses to write a `..` entry — write-side defense in depth). 28 backup tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-03** Call `crate::skills::creator::validate_skill_id(&manifest.id)?` before `target_skills_dir.join(&manifest.id)` in `install_from_local` — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/skills/installer.rs` — *test:* id of `"../etc/cron.d/x"` returns `Err` — *origin:* A-07 — ✅ **DONE:** replaced the weak empty-only check with `super::creator::validate_skill_id` (allows only `[a-zA-Z0-9_-]`, ≤64 → rejects `..`/`/`/`\`/empty) before the id becomes a path component. Tests: `install_from_local_rejects_path_traversal_id` + updated empty-id test. 13 installer tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-04** Create `escape_like(s: &str) -> String` escaping `\`, `%`, `_` and add `ESCAPE '\\'` to every LIKE clause in `forget.rs` and `regions.rs` — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/memory/mod.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/memory/forget.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/memory/regions.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/memory/embeddings.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/cli/memory.rs` — *test:* `--forget "%"` does not delete entire memory tier — *origin:* A-08, A-44 — ✅ **DONE:** shared `memory::escape_like` (escapes `\`/`%`/`_`) + `... COLLATE NOCASE LIKE ?n ESCAPE '\'` across all forget tiers, `wipe_by_source_ref_pattern`, both `recall_from_region` branches, and the `cli/memory` dry-run counts (preview now matches the real delete). Test `forget_escapes_like_wildcards_no_mass_delete` proves `%`/`_` are literal. 304 memory tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-05** Wrap `apply_patch_via_worktree` (sync, calls `std::process::Command` + `std::thread::sleep`) in `tokio::task::spawn_blocking`; replace `std::thread::sleep` with `tokio::time::sleep` inside spawn_blocking — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/coding/dispatcher.rs` — *test:* `cargo clippy -- -D clippy::await_holding_lock` passes; `tokio::time::timeout` in tests does not deadlock — *origin:* A-05 — ✅ **DONE:** the apply call is now `spawn_blocking(move || apply_patch_via_worktree(&task_c,&outcome_c,&cfg_c))` (all three args are `Clone`, so the `Send+'static` closure never captures the `!Send` conn). The git subprocesses + `run_worktree_tests` poll-loop run off the executor; `std::thread::sleep` is KEPT (it is correct on a blocking thread — `tokio::time::sleep` would need an async ctx). `dispatch_session_with_apply` is only awaited (never spawned) so the conn-across-await is fine. 33 dispatcher tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-06** Move post-recall Hebbian writes in `cli/recall.rs:239` inside `spawn_blocking` so SQLite writes never execute on async task thread — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/cli/recall.rs` — *test:* clippy no `await_holding_lock`; recall round-trip integration test — *origin:* A-05, A-66 — ✅ **DONE:** the Hebbian reinforcement loop (N synchronous SQLite UPDATEs) now runs INSIDE the recall `spawn_blocking` block; the conn stays on the blocking thread (no longer returned out) and only `(rows, reinforcements)` cross back — the async side does just the WAL audit emit. `RecallTaskOutput` type alias added (clippy type_complexity). 114 recall tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.

Tests: public host / `8.8.8.8` / link-local `169.254` / userinfo-smuggle `localhost@evil.com` / public IPv6 all rejected; loopback+LAN+ULA accepted. Fixed a stray-underscore port typo in a probe test. 20 omi tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-08** Add LRU eviction (or sweep entries older than `REFILL_PERIOD`) to `rate_limit.rs::allow` to prevent unbounded HashMap growth — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/daemon/rate_limit.rs` — *test:* after 10 000 unique senders, map size stays bounded — *origin:* A-18 — ✅ **DONE:** `evict_if_needed` after each `allow` — high/low-watermark (`MAX_BUCKETS=10000` → trim to `LOW_WATER=7500`): O(1) fast-path until the cap, then one TTL-sweep (drop idle/fully-refilled buckets) + keep-most-recent. Memory hard-capped regardless of attacker sender_id flooding. `bucket_count()` accessor added. Tests: 12.5k unique-sender flood stays ≤ MAX_BUCKETS (0.05s); small load keeps all. 9 rate_limit tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-09** Add `is_cloud_egress() -> bool` to `ProviderKind` and replace the partial match arms in `init.rs`, `jobs.rs`, and `serve.rs` with it; include `AnthropicApi` and `Cohere` — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/consent.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/cli/jobs.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/cli/init.rs` — *test:* `is_cloud(AnthropicApi)` returns true; jobs verdict gates anthropic/cohere — *origin:* A-25 — ✅ **DONE:** the canonical classifier already existed (`consent::is_cloud`) — made it an EXHAUSTIVE match (new ProviderKind variants now fail to compile until classified) and routed the two divergent sites through it: `jobs.rs` cost/consent verdict (its inline set silently MISSED `anthropic_api`+`cohere_api` → those metered jobs escaped the gate) and `init.rs` step8 inline-consent pre-grant (same omission → operators on those providers hit an opaque consent-failure on first chat). Pin-test now covers ALL 11 variants. NOTE: serve.rs:3174 is a ProviderKind→slug *string* map (ARCH dedup C-12 / GOLD-ARCH), not a cloud gate — out of this security scope. 45 consent + 8 jobs tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-10** Default `require_signature = true` on manual update path in `cli/update.rs:218`; allow `--allow-unsigned` opt-out; update any docs claiming updates are signed until CI keypair is provisioned — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/cli/update.rs` — *test:* unsigned release is rejected by default path — *origin:* A-22 — ✅ **DONE:** manual `--self --apply` now passes `require_signature = !--allow-unsigned` (default true). Since `PINNED_PUBKEY` is still None (CI keypair unprovisioned), a default apply fails the gate with a clear, actionable message pointing at `--allow-unsigned` (trusted-network opt-out) rather than a cryptic bail. The require-bails-without-key path is unit-pinned in `sig_verify` (`required_always_bails_without_provisioned_signing`). docgen regenerated for the new flag. 139 updater tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-11** Add `take(MAX_DECOMPRESS_BYTES)` wrappers before decompression in `wal/compress.rs` and `updater/self_update.rs` (xz + gz paths) — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/wal/compress.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/updater/self_update.rs` — *test:* 300 MiB zstd stream is rejected; WAL segment decompression respects quota — *origin:* A-29 — ✅ **DONE:** `wal/compress.rs` decompress now streams through `zstd::stream::read::Decoder` with a 256 MiB cap (`decompress_frames_capped`, read-one-past to detect overflow). `self_update` extract: `MAX_EXTRACT_BYTES`=1 GiB applied to zip (`copy(entry.take())`), gz (`read_to_end(take())`), xz (`LimitedWriter` since the streaming write side has no `Read::take`), and the tar member copy. Test `decompress_rejects_output_over_cap` (tiny-cap, no 256 MiB alloc). 6 compress + 139 updater tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-12** Wrap `DecryptedChromeCredential.password` and Firefox decrypt output in `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>` / `SecretString` so credentials are zeroed on drop — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/credentials/chrome_linux.rs`, `chrome_macos.rs`, `chrome_windows.rs`, `firefox.rs`, `chrome.rs` — *test:* 207 credentials tests pass; scrub enforced by `Drop` + `buf.zeroize()` — *origin:* A-32, A-86 — ✅ **DONE:** `DecryptedChromeCredential` (all 3 OS) now `impl Drop` zeroizing `password` — these live in a `Vec` until mapped into `SecretBytes`, so the long-lived plaintext is now scrubbed on drop. The in-place AES-CBC decrypt buffers are `buf.zeroize()`d (chrome linux/macos + firefox; windows is AES-GCM, no in-place buf). Consumers switched move→`.clone()` (Drop forbids field-move E0509; the clone is immediately scrubbed by `SecretBytes::from_string`). NOTE: kept the field as `String`+Drop rather than `SecretString` to preserve `PartialEq/Eq` + tests; this delivers scrub-on-drop but not `mlock` — a `SecretString` migration (with mlock) is a future hardening on the soon-to-be-feature-gated browser-import path (SEC-18). 207 credentials tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.

## WS-B — Honesty / Truth-in-Advertising (GOLD-HON-NN)

Doc/claim ≠ code fixes AND wiring the truthful behavior.

- [x] **GOLD-HON-01** Downgrade `neoth-migrate` README and `RUNBOOK_phase3_cutover.md` text to "dry-run/Preview only; apply is post-v1.0" — *files:* `SRC/neoth-migrate/src/main.rs` (clap help text), relevant docs — *test:* `neoth-migrate apply --help` says "preview only" — *origin:* B-01, A-02 — ✅ **DONE:** the code was already honest (`run_apply` bails "Memory import (apply) is not yet available"), but the help/doc text over-promised ("Actually migrate"). The `Apply` subcommand doc-comment (= clap `about`) now reads "**Preview only in this release — `apply` is post-v1.0** … validates the manifest then refuses and points you back at `dry-run`"; the module doc (`//!` header + the CLI usage block) is aligned to dry-run/preview-only + apply-post-v1.0. `PLAN/RUNBOOK_phase3_cutover.md` got a top-banner "v1.0 implementation status: only dry-run/preview is shippable; apply/import is post-v1.0 — read the apply steps below as planned design, not a shipped feature." Cargo.toml usage comment marks the `apply` line POST-v1.0. (No separate neoth-migrate README exists — the module doc is the equivalent.) Verified: `cargo run -p neoth-migrate -- apply --help` prints "Preview only in this release — apply is post-v1.0…". neoth-migrate builds + clippy `-D warnings` clean.
- [x] **GOLD-HON-02** Align `operator-journey.md` §5 + FAQ offline table to README's own "Partial" admission that memory-ingest is deferred; remove "Shared memory across devices is the goal here" without marking it as not yet functional — *files:* `docs/operator-journey.md` — *test:* doc review passes — *origin:* B-02
  - ✅ DONE: §5 "Multi-device" cluster bullet reworded — the over-promising "Shared memory across devices is the goal here" replaced with an explicit honest status that mirrors the README feature matrix verbatim: **"Cross-device memory sync is not shipped yet … the private mesh today is Partial — discovery, pairing, the consent gate, and transport config work, but live shared memory across devices (tracked as SL-01) is still in progress. Until it lands each node keeps its own local memory; the cluster shares the channel mesh + node health, not recall."** Line 115 already framed it as a "**Goal:**" (honest); the "one-line map" table row (`| Multi-device | neoth connect, neoth cluster |`) only lists commands, no capability claim — no over-promise there. No standalone "FAQ offline table" exists in the file; the README's `Private mesh = Partial / SL-01 in progress` admission (README.md:284-287) is the alignment source. Doc review passes.
- [x] **GOLD-HON-03** Derive `peer_count` from `registry::load(&home)?.peers.len()` in `cluster.rs::run_status` instead of hardcoded `0usize`; derive `mode`/`policy` from actual config — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/cluster/cluster.rs` — *test:* `neoth cluster status` shows non-zero peers when paired — *origin:* A-13
  - ✅ DONE: (plan-path drift — the function lives in `cli/cluster.rs::run_status`, not `cluster/cluster.rs`.) The status surface lied three ways: `peer_count = 0usize`, `mode = "single-node"`, `policy = "local-only"` were all hardcoded, so `neoth cluster status` reported zero peers / single-node even after `neoth cluster confirm` had paired peers and `cluster.enabled: true` was set (A-13). Now: `peer_count = status_peer_count(&home)?` reads the on-disk registry (`ClusterRegistry::load(&home)?.peers.len()`) — a missing `cluster.yaml` is an honest `0` (load returns the empty default), a **malformed** one propagates as a hard error rather than a false `0`. `mode`/`policy` come from `status_mode_policy(identity.enabled, mdns_enabled, announce_policy.announce_on_untrusted_wifi)` fed by the live `cluster.identity` gate + `policy::load_policy_from_freedom`: off → `single-node`/`local-only`; on → `cluster` + the real announce scope (`discovery-off` / `announce-trusted-wifi-only` / `announce-any-network`). Both helpers are pure + unit-tested (no stdout capture): `status_peer_count_reflects_paired_registry_not_hardcoded_zero` (0 → upsert 2 → 2, the A-13 regression), `status_peer_count_propagates_malformed_registry`, `status_mode_policy_derives_from_config_not_hardcoded` (all 4 posture combos). cli::cluster 3 new + 7066-suite filter / 0 fail, clippy `-p neothd --tests -D warnings` clean.

- [x] **GOLD-COR-04** For security-bearing WAL frames (CHANNEL_PRIVILEGE_BLOCKED, HOOK_BLOCKED, INGEST_EXTRACTED, EMBED_PERSISTED, CONFIG_RELOADED): add `required_audit` flag that returns `Err` to the caller on append failure instead of logging-and-continuing — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/cli/serve.rs` — *test:* WAL writer failure for CHANNEL_PRIVILEGE_BLOCKED surfaces as error to pipeline — *origin:* A-11 — ✅ **DONE (operator-decided 2026-06-06):** new `emit_required_audit()` helper routes all 7 security-frame emit sites (1× CHANNEL_PRIVILEGE_BLOCKED, 3× HOOK_BLOCKED, INGEST_EXTRACTED, EMBED_PERSISTED, CONFIG_RELOADED). A write failure is no longer swallowed at `warn` — it logs at `error!` with a uniform greppable `audit_loss=true` + `event=<name>` field so `neoth monitor`'s rule engine can alert on a lost security record. **Deliberately NOT hard fail-closed** (Alex picked "loud non-silent" over the audit's literal `required_audit→Err`): at every one of these sites the guarded action — block/reject/drop, or persist/reload — has *already completed* by the time the frame is written, so aborting can't undo it (only incoherent post-side-effect state), and since `append` fails on a full WAL quota, propagating would turn a disk-cap into a DoS amplifier on exactly the security paths. Rationale captured in the helper doc-comment. Regression `emit_required_audit_survives_append_failure_without_aborting` (oversize → no panic, no propagate, only the valid frame lands) + existing `…writes_0x3c_frame` happy-path still green. 8 serve tests / 0 fail, clippy clean.

Now it hands the fan-out to a detached `tokio::spawn` and returns 200 immediately; threaded `Arc<WebhookListenerConfig>` through `handle_request`/`handle_meta` so the task can own a clone. To preserve the R2 reviewer's "no unbounded `tokio::spawn`" invariant (the connection permit is released before the pipeline now runs), the detached dispatch acquires a permit from a new `DISPATCH_GATE` semaphore (cap 64) — a fan-out storm queues instead of spawning thousands of provider calls. Regression `meta_post_acks_200_before_pipeline_runs` (pipeline blocks on a 0-permit semaphore; a prompt 200 PROVES detached — the old synchronous code would hang the response forever — and releasing the permit proves fire-and-FORGET, not -drop). **Slack** (`slack_socket`): VERIFIED already correct — `ack(&envelope_id).await?` runs at line 185 BEFORE `dispatch_inbound(...)` at 186, so no retry bug exists there; kept inline (not spawned) to preserve per-chat reply ordering on the single socket. Fixed the way `local_qwen::LoadedModel` already does it: **store** the resolved device on `LoadedOuro` at load time and return *that exact instance* from `model_device()` — NOT a re-derivation. Regression `resolved_device_derives_from_accelerator_field` (all 5 accelerators degrade to Cpu on the non-GPU CI build, mirroring `local_qwen`'s `device_for_falls_back_to_cpu_on_disabled_features`); the 7 weight-gated integration tests now exercise the real GPU path. 12 ouro::adapter tests / 0 fail (7 ignored), clippy `--tests` clean.

- [x] **GOLD-COR-31** Verify `clear_kv_cache()` placement in `ouro/forward.rs` and `ouro/quantized_forward.rs`; move to once-per-`forward` call if correct — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/providers/ouro/forward.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/providers/ouro/quantized_forward.rs` — *test:* real-weight inference test (mark `#[ignore]` + document in test) — *origin:* C-23 — ✅ **DONE (verified + real bug fixed):** A 3-lens research gremium (code-semantics + reference-architecture + fix-options) + the source-paper **arXiv:2510.25741 "Scaling Latent Reasoning via Looped Language Models"** settled this. **The per-loop `clear_kv_cache` is CORRECT and must stay** — the paper confirms prefill needs a *fresh* KV cache per recurrent step (reuse degrades >10 GSM8K points), and removing it would make loop iteration N re-concat the same positions (corrupt double-attention). So the task's suggested "move to once-per-forward" is **wrong** and was NOT done. The real defect is in the **adapter decode loop**: it did true incremental decoding (1 token + growing `seqlen_offset`), but the per-loop clear wipes the cross-call cache at `loop_idx=0` of every decode step, so each new token attended only to ITSELF — silently context-blind, incoherent generation (no crash; mask is `None` for `seq_len==1`). Fixed via **full-resequence decode** (`adapter.rs`: new pure `decode_context_ids` feeds prompt + all generated tokens at `seqlen_offset=0` each step; `forward()` narrows to the last token internally → correct logits over full context). Zero model-file changes, routes through the already-tested full-sequence forward path. The paper's perf-optimal **two-tier "last-step-reuse" persistent cache is the follow-up GOLD-COR-36** (needs real-weight validation, deferred per verify-before-done). Tests: `decode_context_ids_feeds_full_running_sequence_not_single_token` (runnable, proves full-context decode) + `local_ouro_decode_is_context_aware_not_degenerate_repetition` (`#[ignore]` real-weight regression via the existing `NEOTH_OURO_TEST_REPO_PATH` harness). providers::ouro 85/0 (8 ignored), clippy `-D warnings` clean.

Wrapped the particle stripping in the same fixpoint `loop { before = out; for particle … ; if out == before break }` that `clean_topic_de` already uses (out switched from `&str` to owned `String` for the before/after compare). Single-particle cases unchanged; the existing "Remember when we discussed memory tiers" test now yields a cleaner "memory tiers" (still `contains("memory")`). Regressions: `clean_topic_en_peels_chained_openers_via_fixpoint` ("when we talked about rust" → "rust", + single-particle + over-strip guards) and `detects_english_chained_opener_peels_to_bare_topic` (end-to-end `detect_recall_intent("Do you remember when we talked about rust?")` → topic == "rust", was "talked about rust"). recall::conversational 21 / 0 fail, clippy `--tests` clean.
- [x] **GOLD-COR-33** Acquire DB lock only for synchronous DB ops in `serve.rs`; release before any `.await` in `ConnBorrow::Shared` pipeline path (CR-010) — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/cli/serve.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/profile/runner.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/profile/mod.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/cli/chat.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/cli/profile.rs` — *test:* concurrent channel requests do not serialize on DB lock — *origin:* CR-010, A-15 — ✅ **DONE (scope corrected by research):** the shared `views.db` tokio-Mutex was held across the WHOLE `profile::run_pipeline` (incl. its seconds-long LLM extract) in the serve.rs `ConnBorrow::Shared` path, serializing every channel's post-reply profile pipeline on the DB lock. Research established `run_pipeline`'s LLM stage (`extract_delta`) does NOT touch `conn` (works on the in-memory attributed window), so the lock can be scoped to the brief sync DB stages. A naive per-call Owned connection was rejected: `run_pipeline` runs `replay_once` (WAL→views) first and concurrent connections would race the replay offset — the shared connection serializes replay+writes. Fix: `run_pipeline` now takes a `PipelineConn` enum (`Owned(&mut Connection)` for chat/CLI/tests — a zero-cost reborrow; `Shared(&Arc<Mutex<Connection>>)` for the daemon) and `lock()`s the connection only around stages 1 (window read), 5 (redactions read), 5b (gated approval), 6 (apply write) — releasing it around the LLM extract. serve.rs takes a short lock for `replay_once` then passes `Shared`; the `ConnBorrow` enum is gone. The 14 Owned call sites (chat.rs, profile.rs, 12 runner tests) changed to `PipelineConn::Owned`; `run_pipeline`'s future stays `!Send` (rusqlite conn across awaits) so the daemon still drives it under `block_in_place`. Tests: all 17 existing run_pipeline tests pass unchanged (logic preserved) + new `cor33_shared_conn_lock_released_during_llm_extract` (a `BlockingLlmMock` blocks mid-extract; the test proves `shared.lock()` is acquirable while the LLM is in flight — red-before/green-after via `join!`, run_pipeline's future being `!Send` rules out `tokio::spawn`). profile::runner 18/0, cli::serve 8/0, cli::profile 36/0, clippy `-D warnings` clean.
- [x] **GOLD-COR-34** Wrap all 11 remaining detached `tokio::spawn` in `cli/serve.rs` in JoinSet for graceful shutdown and WAL drain — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/cli/serve.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/channels/webhook_listener.rs` — *test:* `SIGTERM` allows WAL drain before exit — *origin:* CR-011 — ✅ **DONE (task premise corrected by a 2-lens research gremium):** there are **NO** truly-detached `tokio::spawn` in serve.rs — all 11 direct spawns are bound to named handles and already `.abort()`+`.await`-ed in the shutdown block. The ONE real fire-and-forget WAL-writing spawn is the **COR-08 `DISPATCH_GATE` Meta fan-out in `webhook_listener.rs:415`** (its own comment deferred the drain to COR-34). Those dispatch tasks hold a `WalWriterHandle` clone via their `cfg` Arc, so `writer_join.await` currently blocks on them *accidentally* — and unboundedly (a hung provider turn hangs daemon exit). Fix: added a shared `dispatch_join: Option<Arc<Mutex<JoinSet<()>>>>` to `WebhookListenerConfig`; `handle_meta` spawns each fan-out INTO it (legacy detached spawn kept when `None`); serve.rs creates the JoinSet, wires it into the WhatsApp listener cfg, and **drains it with a 10s timeout (then `JoinSet::shutdown` abort) BEFORE `drop(writer)`** so in-flight pipeline WAL frames land deterministically and a stuck turn can't hang exit. Also fixed the adjacent `audit_rpc_task` `.abort()`-without-`.await` (handle dropped mid-run). Noted follow-ups (NOT folded in — separate scope): the WhatsApp listener's `std::future::pending` shutdown future (HTTP connections dropped cold) + `n8n_api_task.await` has no timeout. Test `cor34_dispatch_join_tracks_fanout_and_drain_completes_wal_write` (CI-runnable, no signal: posts a Meta webhook whose pipeline writes a marker frame → asserts the JoinSet drain joins ≥1 task AND the marker survives in the WAL). webhook_listener 15/0, cli::serve 8/0, clippy `-D warnings` clean.
- [x] **GOLD-COR-35** Fix `GLOBAL_HLC` test-pollution: `wal/builder.rs` HLC tests (`hlc_logical_increments_within_same_physical_tick`, `hlc_physical_advances_when_clock_moves_forward`) saturate the process-global `GLOBAL_HLC` to `u64::MAX`, poisoning every later `HeaderBuilder::build()` so `collect_proof`'s `ts < end_ns` window-filter drops them — 3 `cli::wal` proof tests fail under the broad `wal::` filter (pass in isolation). Serialize these tests via `crate::test_env::lock` + save/restore `GLOBAL_HLC` so the saturation is contained — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/wal/builder.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/cli/wal.rs` — *test:* full `cargo test --lib wal::` is green (no isolation-dependent failures) — *origin:* discovered Session 40 during GOLD-COR-06 — ✅ **DONE:** added an RAII `HlcRestore` guard (snapshots `GLOBAL_HLC` on construct, restores on drop — panic-safe) + `crate::test_env::lock()` to both saturating `builder.rs` tests; the guard drops (restores) BEFORE the lock guard releases, so a waiting test only ever observes a sane clock. The 3 victim `cli::wal` proof tests now also take the same lock so they never build frames during a saturation window. Both `<`-vs-`u64::MAX` semantics + production code left untouched (test-only isolation fix). Broad `wal::` filter: 307/0 across 3 consecutive runs (was deterministically 3-failed), clippy clean.
- [x] **GOLD-COR-36** Ouro perf: implement the paper's two-tier "last-step-reuse" KV cache (arXiv:2510.25741) so decode is O(n) again instead of the COR-31 full-resequence O(n²) — add a cross-call persistent `cross_cache` (final-loop K/V per past token) alongside the per-loop intra-call scratch; promote the new token's final-loop K/V after the last recurrent iteration; thread an `is_last_loop` signal into `OuroAttention::forward`; also fix the latent `seq_len>1 && seqlen_offset>0` causal-mask shape mismatch surfaced by the COR-31 gremium — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/providers/ouro/layers.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/providers/ouro/quantized_layers.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/providers/ouro/forward.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/providers/ouro/quantized_forward.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/providers/ouro/adapter.rs` — *test:* real-weight context-coherence parity vs the COR-31 full-resequence baseline + a synthetic non-zero-weight context-sensitivity test — *origin:* COR-31 follow-up (perf), arXiv:2510.25741 — ✅ **DONE (3 commits `e9fd746`+`f39f578`+this).** **Verify-first + a 4-lens schema'd gremium (`w1lhceog3`) resolved a self-CONTRADICTORY task premise:** it demanded BOTH "identical logits vs the COR-31 baseline" AND the paper's "last-step-reuse O(n)" — mutually exclusive. Last-step-reuse caches ONLY the final loop, so non-final loops attend over stale final-loop K/V → an APPROXIMATION, not identical. **The identical+O(n) design is a PER-LOOP cache** (NOT the task's single-cross_cache+is_last_loop): each attention holds `kv_caches: Vec<Option<(K,V)>>` of length `total_ut_steps`, indexed by `loop_idx`. Past tokens (0..t-1) at loop L are causally independent of token t, so a past token's loop-L K/V is deterministically identical across decode steps → caching per-loop lets incremental decode (1 new token at a growing `seqlen_offset`) compute BIT-IDENTICAL attention inputs to full-resequence in O(n) forward passes (vs O(n²)). `forward_loops` drops the inner per-loop clear; resets all slots only at `seqlen_offset==0` (so full-resequence@0 is unchanged — the mask "fix" was a non-issue: the existing `causal_attention_mask` already handles offset>0). Native + quantized both migrated (else Q8 silently stays O(n²)). Adapter `run_ouro_forward` gains the O(n) single-token decode gated behind `NEOTH_OURO_KV_CACHE_MODE=per_loop`, **default = the proven COR-31 full_resequence** until a real-weight run certifies the candle tensor-cat path (promotion to a `freedom.yaml::ouro.kv_cache_mode` default-flip is the follow-up). **THE ORACLE (the key insight): the existing synthetic harness uses ZERO weights → a parity test on it is VACUOUS** (degenerate context-independent output catches no mask/RoPE bug). Built NON-ZERO deterministic weights so the model is genuinely context-sensitive (asserted), then `per_loop_cache_decode_matches_full_resequence_baseline` (native + quantized) proves `forward([1,2,3,4]@0)` == `[forward([1,2,3]@0); forward([4]@3)]` within 1e-4. Plus the `#[ignore]` `local_ouro_per_loop_decode_matches_full_resequence_on_real_weights` cert via `NEOTH_OURO_TEST_REPO_PATH`. providers::ouro 85/0 (9 ignored), full lib suite 7033 passed (only the `C:\Temp` env-quirk red), clippy `-D warnings` clean. **WS-C COMPLETE (36/36).**

A `WHERE channel=? AND sender_id=?` scope returns ZERO rows; no-scope returns EVERY operator's text. The text-bearing rows are fundamentally un-attributable to a sender. **So the boundary is enforced at the RECIPIENT, not in SQL:** new pure `recall::channel_recall_authorized(sender_uuid, operator_uuid)` serves channel recall ONLY to the **provable operator** (`inbound.human_uuid == config.channel_weights.operator_human_uuid`, both `Some`) — deliberately stricter than `channel_weights::learn_factor` (an unpinned operator gets NO channel recall, vs learn's `(_,None)=>true`). `human_uuid` is resolved **server-side** from the platform-verified `(channel, sender_id, chat_id)` triple (`channels/identity.rs`) — NOT attacker-supplied — so a sender can't spoof the operator's uuid without authenticating as them on-platform. A non-operator (or unpinned-operator) recall-shaped prompt falls through to the normal LLM turn (no memory read out). **Egress gate (b):** extracted the inline egress tail into a shared `release_channel_reply()` helper (PreEgress hooks → ChannelSend gate, lease-aware, FailClosed → CHANNEL_EGRESS) used by BOTH the recall short-circuit AND the normal model reply — so a no-provider reply is gated **identically** (no policy drift); a gate-Deny / PreEgress-Block returns `Ok(None)` and writes NO CHANNEL_EGRESS (no false attestation). - [x] **GOLD-WIRE-05** Wire `claude_pid_hunter::scan_stuck_processes` into `cli/doctor.rs` as a new check entry; add doctor check that detects deadlocked claude processes — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/cli/doctor.rs`, `SRC/neothd/src/providers/claude_pid_hunter.rs` — *test:* stuck claude process surfaces as WARN in doctor output — *origin:* _wiring_results.md claude_cli_recovery — ✅ **DONE.** New `neoth doctor` check `check_stuck_claude_processes` (registered in `run_all_checks` as #30) runs the new sync `claude_pid_hunter::scan_stuck_processes_blocking` (the doctor registry is sync; the existing async `scan_stuck_processes` keeps its `spawn_blocking` for daemon callers) and renders via a pure `stuck_processes_outcome(&[StuckProcess]) -> CheckOutcome` (empty→PASS, any→WARN listing pid + idle-minutes; never FAIL — a hung process is recoverable). Gated on top-level `provider_kind == claude_cli` (parity with the tmux check; documented scope limit in the CheckDoc: a per-hemisphere-only claude_cli slot isn't scanned yet). Added a CHECK_DOCS runbook entry (`--explain`/`--list-checks`) + bumped both 29→30 count-pins. **The adversarial 2-lens review (correctness + honesty) caught a real latent bug the wiring EXPOSED:** `scan_blocking` did two `sysinfo` refreshes back-to-back with no delay, but sysinfo 0.32 needs `MINIMUM_CPU_UPDATE_INTERVAL` (200ms) between them or `cpu_usage()` reads ~0 for EVERY process → every claude past the 15-min floor would be false-flagged stuck. Fixed with `thread::sleep(MINIMUM_CPU_UPDATE_INTERVAL)` between the refreshes (off-reactor via `spawn_blocking` on the daemon path; only the idle doctor-CLI thread on the sync path). **Honesty:** the doctor output references only real recovery (`kill`/`taskkill`), never the module's `stuck_hint()` which named the unbuilt `neoth doctor stuck-clean` / `neoth chat reset --force` — and (review NIT) I rewrote `stuck_hint()` to drop those phantom commands (now points at the real `neoth doctor` WARN + manual kill), flipped its test from PINNING the phantoms to GUARDING against them, and de-lied the module + `StuckProcess` struct docs. *tests:* `stuck_processes_outcome_warns_and_lists_pid` (WARN+pid, asserts no phantom cmds), `_passes_when_none`, `check_stuck_claude_processes_skips_when_not_claude_cli`, `stuck_hint_points_at_real_recovery_only`. cli::doctor 76/0, claude_pid_hunter 13/0, clippy `-D warnings` clean.

The single `send_and_wait_with_timeouts` in `complete_tmux_uncached` is now inside a classify-driven `loop`: a pure `plan_tmux_retry(signal, attempt) -> TmuxRetryPlan` (Retry{step} | Surface{class,hint}) classifies each failure and the loop honours it — **Auth surfaces immediately** (`max_attempts=0`, never retries), **EmptyStdout / a vanished pane retry once** (the latter killing + rotating the session under a fresh `rotation_seq` name), **Transient retries with exponential backoff** — every class bounded by `max_attempts` (≤4 sends total). Outcome→`FailureSignal` mapping: empty `Ok` / `HardTimeoutNoOutput` → `exit_code=Some(0)` (EmptyStdout); `PaneDisappeared` → its Display carries the SessionCollision needle; `Tmux(e)` → `e.to_string()` (Auth-keyword-or-transient). The slot lock is held across retries by design (the warm pane is a single serial conversation — concurrent callers already queue). **Adversarial review caught + I fixed a real HIGH:** the SessionCollision reset killed the old session but did NOT bump `rotation_seq`, so the respawn reused the dead session's name → tmux `new-session` could fail on the lingering `remain-on-exit` window; now every session-drop rotates (mirrors the compaction path). Also fixed 2 MEDIUMs (removed a now-dead post-loop `response.is_empty()` bail; `plan_tmux_retry` now carries the class in both arms so the surface path doesn't re-`classify_failure`) + a NIT (error arms reuse the variant Display). *tests:* `plan_tmux_retry_{auth_surfaces_immediately, empty_stdout_retries_once_with_backoff, pane_disappeared_resets_and_retries_once, transient_uses_growing_backoff_to_three}` (the pane test feeds the REAL `ClaudeTmuxError::PaneDisappeared` Display). providers::claude_cli 46/0, clippy `-D warnings` clean.

- [ ] **GOLD-ARCH-02** Decompose `cli/chat.rs::run_chat_with` (2082 LOC): extract `build_prompt_bundle`, `enforce_preflight`, `dispatch_provider`, `run_post_reply_pipelines` phase functions — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/cli/chat.rs` — *test:* each phase function has unit tests; integration test confirms same behavior — *origin:* C-02, D-02, CQ-001
- [x] **GOLD-ARCH-03** Extract `wal::scan::for_each_frame(seg_bytes, cb)` handling v1/v2+zstd uniformly; migrate all 57+ callers (incl. `rollback.rs`, `indexer.rs`, `undo.rs`, `updater.rs`) — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/wal/` (new module), 57+ callers — *test:* v2/zstd segment frames are not silently skipped in indexer and rollback — *origin:* C-09, D-01, A-65 — ✅ **DONE** (3 commits: part 1 foundation+indexer, part 2 rollback+undo, part 3 the rest). New `wal::scan::for_each_frame(seg_bytes, cb)` decodes v1 (60 B header) AND v2/zstd (61 B header + decompressed blob) uniformly via `compaction::logical_segment_bytes`, so a compressed segment's frames are read instead of silently yielding ZERO. **Verify-first corrected the "57+ callers" gross overcount** — the real production frame-scanners are ~16; the rest of the `SEGMENT_HEADER_LEN` hits are test fixtures, the constant def, writer header-writes, or already-v2-correct (`cli/verify.rs`, and `cli/wal.rs::{show,collect_proof}` which already do `parse_segment_header`+`decompress_frames`). Migrated read-only scanners to `for_each_frame`: `memory/indexer.rs::replay_once` (kept its resume-cursor walk on logical bytes), `cli/rollback.rs::{find_snapshot_at,collect_snapshots}`, `cli/undo.rs`, `cli/updater.rs::load_results_from_wal`, `cli/checkpoint.rs::find_latest_snapshot`, `cli/kanban.rs`, `permissions/audit.rs::audit_segment`, `cron/runner.rs::aggregate_profile_snapshot`, `daemon/monitor_cron.rs::{count_crc_frames_in_segment,latest_channel_activity_in_segment}`; and `cli/wal.rs::collect_stats` + `cli/hooks.rs::collect_hook_trace` adopted the in-file `parse_segment_header`+`decompress_frames` idiom (they need a torn-tail `bad_frames` count / a `limit` early-stop that `for_each_frame` abstracts away). **`wal/recovery.rs::scan_tail` is the SPECIAL case — deliberately NOT migrated to `for_each_frame`:** its returned offset drives a physical `set_len` TRUNCATION, so it must walk RAW frames + return RAW-FILE offsets. Verify-first established the seal model: production WAL is always v1 (`wal::spawn`→`CompressionPolicy::None`; Zstd3 is test-only), and even under Zstd3 the LIVE segment scan_tail resumes has a RAW body (compression happens only at clean finalize, after which the segment is rotated away, never resumed) — so decompressing it (the flag is set but the body is raw) would ERROR and is wrong. The real latent bug there was the hardcoded 60-byte start offset misaligning a 61-byte v2 header → fixed by deriving `header_len` from `parse_segment_header`. **`wal/redact.rs::scan_and_redact` (privacy guarantee for `memory::forget`):** the hardcoded 60 offset would magic-mismatch on a live-v2 segment, break the loop, and SILENTLY scrub nothing while the caller reports success — a privacy hole; fixed by deriving `header_len` AND refusing (loud error) a *sealed* compressed segment (distinguished from a live-v2 raw body by peeking for a frame MAGIC at `header_len`). The decompress→redact→recompress rewrite for sealed segments is the genuine multi-day deferral → `GOLD-ARCH-03b` (`// TODO` in place). `cluster/wal_sync.rs::collect_gossipable_frames` audited + documented as correct-by-caller (the caller derives the right `header_len` and skips compressed segments; gossip needs raw wire bytes + a cursor, so `for_each_frame` is intentionally NOT used). `cli/migrate.rs` left as-is (v1→v2 tool, guarded `== V1` so the 60-byte slice is sound by construction). **Tests:** `wal::scan` 5 (incl. `compressed_v2_yields_all_frames` — the keystone); regression tests proving v2 frames are seen / privacy holds: indexer `replay_indexes_frames_from_a_v2_compressed_segment`, rollback+undo v2-segment tests, recovery `scan_v2_live_segment_*` (2), redact `scan_redacts_matching_frames_in_a_v2_live_segment` + `scan_refuses_to_redact_a_sealed_compressed_segment`, wal `stats_counts_frames_in_a_v2_compressed_segment`, hooks `collect_hook_trace_reads_frames_from_a_v2_compressed_segment`. Full lib suite **7110 passed**, the only 2 reds are pre-existing + unrelated (WIRE-11's stale `cli-commands.md` + the `C:\Temp\Cargo.toml` env quirk); clippy `-p neothd --tests --no-deps -D warnings` clean.
- [ ] **GOLD-ARCH-03b** Redact sealed compressed (v2/zstd) WAL segments: extend `wal::redact::scan_and_redact` with a decompress → redact-in-logical-space → recompress → atomic-`.tmp`-rename path so `memory::forget` can scrub PII from a finalised compressed segment — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/wal/redact.rs` — *test:* a topic in a sealed v2 segment is zeroed + the segment re-seals + re-decompresses clean — *origin:* GOLD-ARCH-03 split — **DEFERRED (real file-rewrite design):** the GOLD-ARCH-03 redact fix made live-v1/v2 segments correct and **refuses (loud error) a sealed compressed segment** rather than silently no-op'ing (the prior hardcoded-offset bug was a privacy hole — it scrubbed nothing while reporting success). In-place seek+overwrite cannot touch frames inside a zstd blob; redacting one needs a full decompress → mutate → recompress → atomic rewrite (matching `writer::finalize_compressed_segment`'s shape), which is its own slice. Production WAL is uncompressed today (`CompressionPolicy::None`), so no sealed segment reaches `forget` until compression is enabled — the refusal keeps the gap honest in the meantime.

- [x] **GOLD-ARCH-10** Cache compiled `Regex` in `HookDef` via `OnceLock`; reuse for both `fires` check and `Replace` action (currently double-compiles per dispatch) — *files:* `SRC/neothd/src/hooks/dispatcher.rs` — *test:* `hooks_dispatch_benchmark` shows 2× improvement — *origin:* A-40, C-24 — ✅ **DONE:** new `compile_cached(pattern) -> Result<Regex, regex::Error>` — a process-wide `OnceLock<Mutex<HashMap<String, Regex>>>` memo keyed by pattern string. **Design note (verify-first):** a per-`HookDef` `OnceLock<Regex>` field (the task's literal suggestion) is impossible — `HookDef` derives `Clone` and `OnceLock<Regex>` is not `Clone` — so the memo lives at module scope instead, which is strictly better (shared across ALL hooks, not just within one HookDef, and hook patterns are a bounded static config set so the map can't grow unbounded). Both compile sites — the `fires` matcher check AND the `Replace` action — now call it, killing the per-dispatch double-compile + recompile. ### Deep-read derived adaptations (2026-06-12) — ALL 1.0-GOLD scope

> 6 code-level deep-reads (Alex-00..06 + Jarvis/Veronica/LOWKEY brains). Full evidence (file:line, coverage, license) in `REVIEWS/_gold_audit/_alex_repos_2026-06-12/{openhuman,hermes,jarvis_veronica_lowkey,mnemo,odysseus,rscrypto}.md`. License discipline: odysseus = AGPLv3 → re-implement logic/prompts as fresh Rust, NEVER paste; mnemo = MIT; rscrypto = unaudited private re-impl → do NOT add as dep (steal 3 patterns only); openhuman/hermes/jarvis/veronica/lowkey = operator's own.
> **Highest leverage first:** ADAPT-HERMES-01 (operator crons) · ADAPT-OH-01 (migration-first onboarding) · ADAPT-MEM-01..06 (fact-state-machine, contradiction ledger, parallel recall, DSPM scoring, pre-compact backup, knowledge-graph) · ADAPT-ODY-04/09/06 (stall watchdog, context compactor, incognito) · ADAPT-ODY-05+GOLD-FEAT-08 (refusal→abliterated fallback, TODO:40-43).

#### ▶ Builder's guide — READ THIS before picking any GOLD-ADAPT item

**Per-item workflow:** (1) Open the source evidence `REVIEWS/_gold_audit/_alex_repos_2026-06-12/<repo>.md` — it has the exact source `file:line` + the algorithm/prompt to port. (2) **Verify-first** the NEOTH integration point still exists — `grep`/read the named module+fn before trusting any line number (the 2026-06-12 ARCH-01 refactor moved ~1100 LOC; refs drift; design notes can be wrong). (3) Port the LOGIC, not the source (AGPLv3 odysseus: re-implement in Rust, NEVER paste). (4) Add a `#[cfg(test)]` test proving the *test:* acceptance line. (5) `cargo clippy -p neothd --tests --no-deps -- -D warnings` + `cargo test -p neothd --lib` green via the §1 vcvars recipe (feature-gated code also needs `--features X`). (6) Flip `[ ]`→`[x]` here AND in `PROGRESS_v1_0_GOLD.md` in the SAME commit.

**Recurring integration patterns (the "how" for most items — wire to these, don't reinvent):**
- **New daemon cron** (HERMES-06/07/16, MEM-02/07/11/12/15, OH-07): `daemon/<name>_cron.rs` exposing `pub async fn spawn_<name>_cron(cfg, [provider,] writer) -> Option<JoinHandle<..>>` that returns `None` when its `freedom.yaml` flag is off; add a 1-line `serve_tasks::spawn_<name>(...)` delegate; call it in `run_serve`, register the handle in `worker_watch`, abort it in `serve_tasks::shutdown_background_tasks` (preserve abort ORDER). Mirror an existing cron (e.g. `spawn_monitor_cron`, `spawn_drift_alert_cron`).
- **New config field** (OH-11, G-01, LOWKEY-08): add to the right struct in `config/mod.rs` (or a `config/*.rs` domain file) with field-level `#[serde(default)]` so existing `freedom.yaml` still parses; read at the consumer; never required. Add a default + roundtrip + yaml-parse test.
- **New WAL event** (MEM-02/05, plugin events): **5 sites** (§2) — const + compile-time band-assert + `EVENT_NAME_TABLE` + `all_event_codes_are_unique` + emit-site; pick a free slot from §2 (`0x5D-5F`, `0x69-6F`, …) and coordinate the band FIRST.
- **New CLI subcommand** (`neoth cron`, …): enum variant in `cli/mod.rs` + `mod` decl + dispatch arm (inject `global_output`); model it on `cli/fact_check.rs` (WIRE-11). Adding/removing a command → run `NEOTH_REGEN_CLI_DOCS=1 cargo test -p neothd --lib cli_commands_md_is_up_to_date` (§1).
- **GUI feature** (ODY-04/05/09/10/11, OH-08/12): the Slint **3-file pattern** (see the shipped ODY-01/02/03 above for a worked example) — `neothd-gui/ui/*.slint` (component + struct fields + callbacks), `neothd-gui/src/panel_logic.rs` (a serde-decoupled mirror struct + load fn), `neothd-gui/src/main.rs` (ModelRc builder + worker-thread load + `on_*` handler). Backend feeds the GUI by EXTENDING the `cli/chat.rs --stream` sentinel + updating `strip_stream_sentinel` in `main.rs`. `neothd-gui` is a BIN crate → `cargo test -p neothd-gui` (no `--lib`); set `NEOTH_LOG=error` so daemon stdout-tracing can't corrupt the GUI subprocess JSON parser.
- **New memory module** (MEM-01/03/06/07, OH-10): `memory/<name>.rs` + `pub mod` in `memory/mod.rs`; recall integration in `cli/recall.rs::run_recall`; keep GUI-facing structs serde-decoupled; respect the GDPR `forget` cascade (a new table must be wiped by `memory/forget.rs`).
- **New provider** (ODY-15 copilot): `providers/<name>.rs` implementing the provider trait; register in `providers::from_config`; add a wizard step in `cli/init.rs`; honour cost-free-first + the consent gate (`consent::is_granted`) + the n8n consent gate.

**Gotchas (§2 + 2026-06-12 audit):** `&s[..N]` panics on multibyte UTF-8 → `chars().take(N)`; stream/pastejack guards must be `chars()`-based; a default-OFF Cargo feature ships only if the release step adds `--features X`; never hold a SQLite/Mutex guard across `.await`; new blocking `std::fs`/syscalls on the async reactor need `spawn_blocking`; new cloud egress must pass `consent::is_granted` at EVERY autonomy level (not just Strict); `forget --physical` and `verify` must fail-LOUD on tamper-suspect WAL, never silently.

**Build order / dependencies (do the unlockers first):**
1. **HERMES-01 (operator crons)** is the keystone — once it lands, HERMES-06/07/16 + MEM-16 become *operator-cron prompts* (effort S, not L).
2. MEM-06 (knowledge graph) before MEM-14; MEM-01 (fact-state-machine) before MEM-05 + LOWKEY-05 groundtruth checks; MEM-04 (5-LOC scoring) is the easiest first win.
3. ODY-05 (metrics chip) consumes HERMES-09 (metering); OH-01 (migration) pairs with GOLD-FEAT-04.
4. **Refusal-fallback bundle** (the TODO:40-43 pain): ODY-08 teacher-escalation + LOWKEY-06 PME + G-03 abliterated model → ship together under GOLD-FEAT-08.

**License per source:** odysseus = AGPLv3 (re-implement, never paste) · mnemo = MIT (port freely) · rscrypto = NO dep (3 patterns as local code only) · openhuman/hermes/jarvis/veronica/lowkey = operator's own.

- **COLLAPSE DUPLICATES (do NOT implement both):** `MEM-07` ≡ `JV-MEM-08` → keep **JV-MEM-08** (fuller live-code spec). `MEM-10` ≡ `JV-MEM-10` → keep **JV-MEM-10**. `MEM-11`+`MEM-12` ≡ `JV-MEM-15`+`JV-MEM-16` → keep **JV variants** (fold NN-MEM-07 + Gremium loops in). `JV-MEM-13` ≡ `JV-SELF-02` (AMEM4Rec, same algo) → keep one.
- **UNIFIED GRAPH (MEM-06 vs JV-MEM-08):** ONE table `idx_memory_graph(from,to,type:entity_relation|hebbian_coAccess,weight,count,...)`. **MEM-06** (mnemo LLM-extract) writes `entity_relation` at ingest; **JV-MEM-08** (Hebbian) writes `hebbian_coAccess` at recall; BFS traverses both. **Order: MEM-06 schema FIRST, JV-MEM-08 adds the hebbian write-path.** Never build two graphs.
- **MERGE:** JV-MEM-07 (length-norm) folds into JV-MEM-06 (same file/test). JV-MEM-04 (Weibull) + NN-MEM-01 (pinned) ship together.

- **BUILD ORDER (5 phases):** ① Foundations: JV-MEM-02 + NN-MEM-01 + (JV-MEM-04⊕JV-MEM-07). ② Data-quality: JV-MEM-01 + (JV-MEM-03⊕NN-MEM-06) + NN-MEM-03/04. ③ Recall: JV-MEM-06 + (MEM-06⊕JV-MEM-08 unified graph) + JV-MEM-10. ④ Ops-intelligence: (MEM-11→JV-MEM-15→NN-MEM-07) + (JV-MEM-16⊕NN-MEM-02) + NN-MEM-05. ⑤ Import/vault: JV-MEM-05/09/11/12/13 + JV-IMP-05.

When both a MEM-* and a JV-MEM-* cover the same ground, the JV-* (live-code) spec wins. ## WS-V — GOLD Verification Findings (external review 2026-06-11, triaged)

**Provenance:** an external static-analysis review (`NEOTH-GOLD-Verifikation-2026-06-11.md`, base `35c94d2` → HEAD `550f5f1`) checked all 148 DONE-claims (fix exists? wired in the live path? closes the origin finding? regressions?) + diffed all 353 changed files. A 22-agent read-only verification Workflow (`wxjetsgrm`, 1.9M tok) then re-checked every finding against current code. **Verdict: 209 CONFIRMED · 23 overstated (review wrong / already-fine) · 24 intentional (documented tradeoff) · 3 unverifiable.** Confirmed severity: 0 critical · 16 high · 61 medium · 94 low · 38 info (59 security-relevant). **The full triaged list — all 209 confirmed + the overstated/intentional record, decisive file:line per finding — is LOCAL-ONLY at `REVIEWS/_gold_audit/GOLD_VERIFICATION_TRIAGE_2026-06-11.md` (gitignored, like the other deep-audit artifacts).** No fixes applied yet (operator decides per finding). *NOTE: `serve.rs`/`serve_tasks.rs` line refs in the source review are stale — ARCH-01 moved ~1100 LOC since; findings were located by content, not line number.*

**Re-opened DONE-claims:** several WS-A/B/C tasks marked `[x]` shipped their NAMED fix but the verification found the origin finding only PARTIALLY closed (or the fix not wired): GOLD-SEC-08 (eviction not-wired → DoS persists), SEC-10/12/15/20/21/23/28/33/35, HON-16, COR-04/09/25, ADOPT-10 + the §1 partials. Their checkboxes stay `[x]` (the named deliverable shipped) — the residual GAP is tracked as the GOLD-SEC-*/GR-*/DD-* items below + in the triage file. This is the honest reconciliation, not a wholesale revert.

### HIGH (16) — confirmed; security 🔒 first
- [x] **GOLD-SEC-08** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — channels::rate_limit bucket map now bounded (MAX_BUCKETS=10_000 + evict_if_needed wired into try_consume; idle-TTL sweep + live_bucket_count accessor + regression test); the A-18 unique-sender flood DoS is closed on the live inbound path. bd068f8.
- [x] **GR-003** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — n8n provider_call cloud-check now routes through the canonical EXHAUSTIVE `consent::is_cloud` (was an inline matches! omitting AnthropicApi+Cohere → those skipped the Strict consent gate). is_some_and(consent::is_cloud); compile-enforced no-drift. n8n_api::handlers 5/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-005** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — `forget` now rebuilds the on-disk HNSW snapshot from the wiped SQLite (new `embeddings::rebuild_snapshot_if_present(conn, home)`, called in run_forget when embedding_rows>0), so forgotten vectors are purged from the searchable index, not just idx_embedding. Best-effort (SQLite already wiped; cold-load falls back). Tests: rebuild_snapshot_if_present_purges_forgotten_vectors + noop-without-snapshot. clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-007** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — verify is no longer fail-open on a corrupt segment header. verify_segments only skipped <SEGMENT_HEADER_LEN stubs; a ≥60-byte segment whose header didn't parse fell through logical_segment_bytes' silent raw@0 fallback → 0 markers found → reported clean (a corrupted-header, esp. compressed, segment evaded detection). Now an unparseable header on a real-sized segment is recorded as a tamper-suspect FAILURE (run_verify then bails). logical_segment_bytes untouched (indexer's legit bare-stream handling preserved). Test verify_flags_a_corrupt_header_segment_as_tamper_suspect; cli::verify 17/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-008** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — forget --physical no longer reports success when a segment refused redaction. The output ignored the errors field (incremented when a sealed/compressed segment refuses redaction or an audit-marker emit fails) and printed the affirmative 'no matching WAL frames — SQLite-only forget was sufficient' / 'GDPR-grade erasure' regardless. Now: errors count printed; an INCOMPLETE warning replaces the success message when errors>0; and the command FAILS LOUD (non-zero exit via bail) so an operator/script never mistakes a partial/refused redaction for a confirmed wipe (pure physical_erasure_incomplete predicate drives both). Test physical_erasure_incomplete_when_any_error; cli::memory 5/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-018** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — SmartApprove confirm-bypass is now per-server: global security.smart_approve is the MASTER switch (allocates the cache), and the bypass fires only when the specific McpServerConfig.smart_approve is ALSO true (gate.rs:277 `cfg.smart_approve && smart_approve_is_readonly`). Enabling it on one trusted server no longer bypasses confirm for the rest. New per-server field (serde default false, 10 literals) + master-switch doc + regression test smart_approve_is_per_server_opt_in. mcp:: 185/0; clippy -D warnings clean.
- [x] **GR-027** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — from_config_for_utility no longer leaks the main provider key to a different utility vendor. Refactored into a pure build_utility_config that strips provider_key/endpoint/model when the utility kind differs from the main kind (require_provider_key then resolves the utility's OWN key or fails cleanly → best-effort caller falls back); same-vendor flagship→fast keeps the shared key. Tests utility_config_strips_main_key_for_a_different_vendor + keeps_key_for_same_vendor; providers:: 587/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-032** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — risk-override leases are now bounded + single-use: (1) dangerous_command/egress grants are hard-capped at MAX_RISK_OVERRIDE_TTL_SECS=24h via LeaseScope::check_ttl, enforced in both 'neoth lease grant' and 'neoth risk-confirm' (--ttl 9999d rejected); (2) a lease that lifts a block is CONSUMED on use (consume_risk_leases revokes+persists in dispatch_loop), so the window authorises exactly ONE blocked call, not unlimited until expiry. Per-command scope is N/A to a pre-auth window — single-use bounds the blast radius. Tests risk_override_scopes_are_ttl_capped + over_cap_ttl_is_rejected + single-use assert; lease 10/0, risk_confirm 6/0, dispatch_loop 17/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-033** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — neoth permissions audit no longer swallows the WAL-scan tamper-suspect error. for_each_frame's Result was 'let _ ='-discarded; it Errs only on an unreconstructable (tamper-suspect) compressed blob, so the audit silently reported a clean trail over a corrupt segment (fail-open). The Result is now propagated with context (audit_segment returns Err → the command fails loud); benign paths unaffected (for_each_frame still breaks cleanly on torn tails). Test audit_segment_fails_loud_on_a_tamper_suspect_compressed_segment; permissions::audit 15/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-043** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — staged-apply no longer bypasses the SEC-10 signature gate: (1) the require-signature + no-pinned-key fail-closed bail now runs BEFORE both staged + fresh paths (was fresh-only, which the staged fast-path returned before reaching); (2) apply_from_staged RE-VERIFIES the minisign signature at apply time vs the in-binary pinned key (stage_update now persists the .minisig; the attacker-writable signature_status in pending.json is no longer trusted) → a planted/swapped staged archive can't be applied. Tests: apply_from_staged_refuses_when_signature_required_but_unverifiable (+no-swap) + tamper + legacy-parse; self_update 61/0, cli::update 12/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-055** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — a deeplink-sourced (untrusted) recipe that injects a 'system' instructions override now requires an explicit operator PREVIEW + confirm before the agentic run (confirm_untrusted_deeplink gate in run_one, fired by the pure deeplink_system_override_needs_confirm predicate); fail-closed when stdin isn't a TTY (refuses rather than running untrusted system instructions unattended). Deeplinks without a system override + local-file recipes are unaffected. Tests deeplink_with_system_override_needs_confirm + confirm_untrusted_deeplink_fails_closed_when_not_a_tty; cli::recipe 7/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-058** 🔒 ✅ FIXED — redact no longer fails-open on a corrupt segment header. The Err arm of the header-len derivation fell back to SEGMENT_HEADER_LEN (60), so a corrupt v2 header (61) misaligned the first frame → MAGIC failed → the loop broke → SILENT null-redaction while memory::forget reported success (privacy hole). Now a segment ≥ SEGMENT_HEADER_LEN whose header doesn't parse is REFUSED loudly (like the sealed-compressed case) → forget surfaces the error (GR-008); a sub-header file keeps the benign no-op. Test scan_refuses_a_segment_with_an_unparseable_header; wal::redact 12/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-002** ✅ FIXED — the auto-promote gate (COR-03) can no longer be bypassed by an empty patch + self-reported green tests. The apply path stamped applied=cfg.test_cmd.is_some() even for an empty patch (which apply_patch_via_worktree returns Ok for WITHOUT running any worktree suite) → a no-op auto-promoted to DONE. Now applied requires BOTH a configured test_cmd AND a non-empty patch (pure apply_is_test_verified); an empty patch stays applied=false → check_auto_promotable blocks it (NotApplied). Test empty_patch_is_never_test_verified; coding::dispatcher 35/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-006** ✅ FIXED — a stale pre-ARCH-03 wal_cursor below the real header_len (e.g. 60 vs a 61-byte v2 header) no longer perpetually stalls the indexer mid-header. The resume offset is clamped up to header_len (start_offset.max(header_len)), so a stale/short cursor self-heals to the first real frame; a normal resume cursor is unchanged. Test replay_self_heals_a_stale_cursor_below_header_len (plants cursor=60 on a 61-byte-header v2 segment → indexes both frames); memory::indexer 6/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **DD-01** ✅ FIXED — import-format.md now states the real command surface up front (status banner: only 'neoth-migrate dry-run --manifest' works today; 'apply' is preview-only/refuses with the real WAL-writing import post-v1.0; 'export' not yet implemented) + the runnable example and §5 export section are corrected/caveated — no longer presents the import/export pipeline as shipped.
- [x] **DD-02** ✅ FIXED — quickstart/install/getting-started.md no longer instruct a bare 'cargo install neoth' (which README:54 denies is published); all three now show the bootstrap installer / from-source 'cargo install --path neothd' with the not-yet-on-crates.io caveat, consistent with README.

- [x] **GR-046** (MED) ✅ FIXED — a confirm_high block is now liftable via 'neoth risk-confirm'. check_risk_leases computed needs_dangerous on Critical only, so a HIGH finding (which generates a Confirm under confirm_high) was never recognized as needing the DangerousCommand lease → unliftable. New pure risk_needs_dangerous_lease(risk, confirm_high) includes High when confirm_high; confirm_high threaded from security_policy. Test confirm_high_makes_a_high_finding_need_the_dangerous_lease; mcp::dispatch_loop 18/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-022** / **GOLD-SEC-29** / **GOLD-SEC-33** (MED · GROUP 11) ✅ FIXED/CLARIFIED — Windows home-ACL check no longer false-positives on every install: icacls echoes the queried PATH (contains \Users) as its leading token; the scan now anchors on the ACE form PRINCIPAL:( (new pure icacls_broad_principal) so only a REAL broad grant matches (GR-022/SEC-29). SEC-33: the Windows warn-only vs Unix hard-fail asymmetry is INTENTIONAL — Windows dirs routinely inherit broad ACEs (verified: C:\Temp grants Jeder/Everyone — my own Err-escalation broke windows_path_no_panic, proving a hard-fail would lock out a legitimate home); the wizard's explicit icacls restriction is the enforcement. Test icacls_broad_principal_ignores_echoed_path_but_catches_aces; daemon::isolation 3/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-047** (MED) ✅ OVERSTATED/already-fixed — the triage read apply_risk_leases in isolation; the FULL chain already makes a risk-confirm grant SINGLE-USE (not TTL-duration) via dispatch_loop.rs:262 consume_risk_leases (revokes+persists the covering lease on the lift — shipped in GR-032). No code change. Verified by the wsv-med-analysis workflow + grep.
- [x] **GR-094** (MED) ✅ FIXED — launch_editor splits $EDITOR/$VISUAL via a quote-aware split_editor_command instead of split_whitespace, so an editor path WITH SPACES (Windows: "C:\Program Files\…\subl.exe" -w) stays one token. No shell invoked → no injection. Test split_editor_command_handles_quoted_paths_with_spaces; cli::editor 7/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-026** / **GR-028** / **GR-076** (MED/INFO · GROUP 9) ✅ FIXED — utility fast-pin overhaul (providers/mod.rs + model_roles.rs, one pass): new resolve_exact (NO flagship fallback) so a provider with no fast row (bedrock/azure/cohere) leaves the model UNSET instead of pinning the expensive flagship (GR-026); pin gated on !is_local_provider so a LOCAL utility isn't pinned the table's bare local id (an invalid HF path) (GR-028); doc corrected to match (GR-076). Tests resolve_exact_has_no_flagship_fallback + utility_config_no_flagship_or_local_pin; providers:: 589/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-034** / **GR-023** (MED) ✅ FIXED — gguf_variants::resolve_live now routes HF GGUF lookups through providers::http_client::build_client() (audited, NEOTH_HTTP_PROXY + egress-allowlist aware) instead of a direct reqwest::Client that bypassed the operator's egress proxy; the tight 8s budget kept as a per-request timeout (overrides build_client's 120s). One fix closes both IDs (same file:line root). models::gguf_variants 9/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-065** (MED) ✅ FIXED — jina_reader uses build_client_no_redirect() (the SX-01 norm) instead of the redirect-following build_client(), so r.jina.ai (a third-party proxy) can't 30x-bounce the fetch to an arbitrary host the SSRF guard never saw. Module doc updated. Test jina_client_does_not_follow_redirects; tools::jina_reader 8/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-042** (MED) ✅ FIXED — the RSS feed poller no longer follows redirects past its own SSRF guard. validate_url checks only the ORIGINAL feed URL, so the redirect-following build_client() let a validated URL 30x-redirect to an internal/metadata target. Swapped to build_client_no_redirect() (the SX-01 norm web_fetch already uses) — the validated URL is the only host fetched. Test rss_client_does_not_follow_redirects; cli::rss_feed_task 11/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-080** (MED) ✅ FIXED (doc) — the SecurityPolicy field doc no longer over-claims egress is 'deny/confirm by default'. Corrected to the real defaults: dangerous-command inspector DENIES a Critical finding by default, but the egress inspector is WARN-ONLY by default (EgressMode::Allow — the intentional ADOPT-23-F5 non-breaking default); operator opts into confirm_unknown/deny_unknown (config/mod.rs:138). Doc-only (compiler-inert comment).
- [x] **GR-107** (MED) ✅ FIXED — the dangerous-command inspector now flags NEOTH's OWN privilege-escalation commands (neoth risk-confirm / lease grant / sudomode / autonomy full|set full) as Critical, so the LLM issuing them via the tool-loop shell can't silently widen its own permissions (which would bypass every other gate). New rule neoth_self_privilege_escalation (inspector scans LLM tool-calls, not the operator CLI). Test flags_neoth_self_privilege_escalation; security::dangerous_command 9/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-038** (MED) ✅ FIXED — 'neoth consent grant/revoke' now enforces the required-audit posture BEFORE the mutation (enforce_required_audit, mirroring cli::autonomy + fs.rs), so a security-relevant consent change can't land without an audit record when the daemon owns the WAL but its audit-RPC listener is unreachable under a required posture. Both render_grant + render_revoke guarded; required read via consent_audit_required (missing/corrupt config → no posture). Test consent_audit_required_reads_the_posture_flag; cli::consent 4/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-099** (MED) ✅ FIXED — 'neoth goal show' no longer hides an active goal/grind behind '(none)' when freedom.yaml exists-but-fails-to-parse. The unwrap_or_default() that swallowed IO+YAML errors is replaced by load_for_show: a MISSING config → fresh-install default (ok), an EXISTING but unparseable one → surfaced error. Test goal_show_surfaces_a_corrupt_config_instead_of_hiding_it; cli::goal 2/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-101** (MED) ✅ FIXED — enabling FULL-AUTO now requires an explicit operator confirmation BEFORE the persist (confirm_full_auto: prints the consequence + interactive y/N), fail-closed when stdin isn't a TTY so the most-permissive mode can never be enabled unattended. Guards BOTH paths: run_set_mode (sudomode/full-auto) + run_set (autonomy set full, on escalation). Test confirm_full_auto_fails_closed_when_not_a_tty; cli::autonomy 10/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-117** (MED) ✅ FIXED — sub-recipe file paths are containment-checked: dir.join(sub.file) is canonicalized and must start_with the canonicalized parent recipe dir, so a sub.file of '../../secret.yaml' (or an absolute path) is REFUSED instead of reading arbitrary files (recipe.rs resolve_subrecipes). Test subrecipe_path_traversal_is_rejected; cli::recipe 8/0; clippy clean.
- [x] **GR-105** (MED) ✅ FIXED — domain_allowed no longer lets a single-label (bare TLD) allowlist entry suffix-match the whole TLD ('com' allowing evil.com); a single-label entry is honoured by EXACT match only, suffix matching requires ≥2 labels (risk_gate.rs:78). Test bare_tld_allowlist_entry_does_not_overmatch; security::risk_gate 15/0; clippy clean.
Full triaged detail (decisive file:line per finding) in the local file. **Security-relevant MEDIUM next-priority cluster:** egress-proxy bypass (GR-023/034 GGUF resolver ignores NEOTH_HTTP_PROXY), SSRF-via-redirect (GR-042 RSS poller follows redirects past `validate_url`; GR-016 jina r.jina.ai off the no_outbound_network allowlist), unrestricted pm-skill `tool_allowlist:[]` = unrestricted + prompts instruct web-egress/file-writes (GR-050/054), consent-grant skips required-audit (GR-038), risk-confirm lifts the whole dimension not the single call (GR-047), relay auth has no client token (GR-009), MCP per-read-not-per-request timeout (GR-015), Windows isolation `\Users` false-positive + warn-only asymmetry (GR-022, SEC-33). The OVERSTATED set (review was wrong) + INTENTIONAL set (deliberate tradeoffs, e.g. SEC-27 opt-out backup) are listed in the triage file so they are NOT re-investigated.

## WS-HR — Headroom Token-Compression Port (GOLD-HR-NN)

**Origin & verdict (2026-06-11, operator P0 "SAU WICHTIG — alle LLM calls"):** [headroom](https://github.com/chopratejas/headroom) (Apache-2.0; Python lib + a full Rust `crates/headroom-core`) compresses tool outputs / logs / diffs / files / RAG chunks **before** they reach the LLM — **60–95% fewer tokens, same answers**. Deep-read of the ACTUAL Rust core (`QUELLEN/headroom/crates/headroom-core/src/transforms/`, gitignored). **Decision: NATIVE Rust port of the pure transform logic into NEOTH** — the self-contained hard rule forbids an external Python proxy, and headroom's Rust core ports near-1:1. **The load-bearing safety idea = CCR (Content-Caching-and-Retrieval): stash the dropped original under an `md5[:24]` key + append an inline retrieval marker, so the model can always pull back what was compressed → lossy compression stays answer-safe.** Plus the **live zone**: never compress the most-recent/active turns (preserves correctness + provider prompt-cache hits). New module `SRC/neothd/src/context/compress/`; config `freedom.yaml::compression`; WAL `COMPRESSION_APPLIED` (bytes_before/after, ratio, kind). Prime integration site = the MCP tool-output path (`mcp/dispatch_loop.rs` `tool_result_blocks`) — the #1 token sink. Grounded findings (Workflow `wf_d975e4c5-136`, partial): DiffCompressor 40–90%, DiffNoise lockfile-offload >90%, LogCompressor 70–99%, LogTemplate (Drain-style) 70–80% — all gated by the CCR retrieval guarantee.

- [ ] **GOLD-HR-00** Operator tooling — install headroom for Claude Code ITSELF (`uv tool install "headroom-ai[proxy]"`) + the `headroom` Claude-Code plugin/hooks (SessionStart/PreToolUse → `headroom init hook ensure`) so the operator's own agent sessions compress context; auto-update via a scheduled `uv tool upgrade headroom-ai`. (Operator-machine tooling, SEPARATE from the NEOTH native port. ⚠ `headroom init` reroutes `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` through a local proxy — reversible, but verify the live session survives before claiming done.) *test:* `headroom --version` resolves; the proxy round-trips a compression on a sample. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-01** `compress/content_detector.rs` — classify a block into {code, log, diff, json, search-result, prose} (port `transforms/content_detector.rs` + `detection.rs`; the magika ML detector is OUT — use the heuristic path). The router prerequisite for every transform. *test:* a cargo log, a git diff, a JSON blob, and a fenced code block each classify correctly. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-02** `compress/pipeline.rs` — the orchestrator + `adaptive_sizer` (pick the target size from the token budget) + `freedom.yaml::compression{ enabled:false (default), min_block_bytes, live_zone_turns }` + the **live-zone guard** (never compress the last N turns / the active tool call). Behaviour-preserving when disabled. *test:* disabled = byte-identical passthrough; a live-zone block is never compressed. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-03** `compress/ccr.rs` — the Content-Caching-and-Retrieval safety store: stash the original under `md5[:24]`, emit the compressed body + a retrieval marker; `neoth ctx retrieve <key>` (and an MCP-visible marker the model can call) pulls back the exact original. The primitive that makes every lossy transform answer-safe. *test:* compress → marker → retrieve round-trips the original byte-for-byte. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-04** `compress/log_compressor.rs` + `log_template.rs` — build/test/cargo output compressor (keep the 5–10 real errors, drop the noise; Drain-style template mining collapses repeated `Compiling crate vX.Y.Z` lines into one template + variant rows). 70–99% on logs. *test:* a 10k-line cargo log with 3 errors → ≤100 lines with all 3 errors retained. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-05** `compress/diff_compressor.rs` + `diff_noise.rs` — git-diff compressor (trim context lines, cap files) + lockfile / whitespace-only-hunk offload (one CCR-retrievable tombstone per file). 40–90% on diffs. *test:* a `Cargo.lock`-dominated diff offloads the lock body; a whitespace-only commit collapses to tombstones; both retrieve. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-06** `compress/smart_crusher/` — the flagship general structured compressor (analyzer → planning → crushers → outliers → compaction) for arbitrary tool output. *test:* a large structured JSON / tool dump compresses ≥50% and retrieves losslessly via CCR. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-07** `compress/{search_compressor,anchor_selector,tag_protector}.rs` — search-result compression + anchor selection (keep the spans that matter) + **tag protection** (NEVER corrupt a tool-call / code-fence / XML boundary mid-compression). *test:* a block with nested ```fences``` and `<tool_call>` tags survives byte-intact at its structural boundaries. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-08** WIRE the pipeline at the prime site — compress `tool_result_blocks` content in `mcp/dispatch_loop.rs` before it's pushed into the prompt (gated on `compression.enabled`, live-zone-aware, CCR-backed) + WAL `COMPRESSION_APPLIED`. *test:* a big tool result is compressed in-prompt, ratio audited; disabled = byte-identical. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-09** WIRE external content — `tools/web_fetch.rs` / `web_extract.rs` output + recall/RAG chunks (`cli/recall.rs` + memory injection) run through the pipeline before prompt assembly. *test:* a fetched HTML→text blob compresses; recall chunks shrink; both retrieve. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-10** Provider-chokepoint final pass + savings metering — a last uniform compression gate before the model call (`providers/` or `enriched_request`), token-savings tied to `UsageMeter` and a `neoth ctx savings` report. *test:* end-to-end savings counter increments; the live zone is never touched. — *origin:* headroom
- [ ] **GOLD-HR-11** Upstream re-sync + attribution — pin the ported headroom version in `NOTICE` (Apache-2.0) + a tracked checklist to re-diff `crates/headroom-core` on upstream releases. *test:* `NOTICE` carries the headroom commit; the doc lists every ported transform. — *origin:* headroom
